All 40 trigger occurrences belong to NM0020583 (Farmington) limit set schedule 3600891019 and share identical DMR comment text, but retrieved evidence shows three mechanically separable classes with different semantic consequences.\n\n## Evidence summary\n\n1. **Limit-set comment propagation (24 rows).** Non-TDS parameters (BOD, TSS, pH, E. coli, flow, etc.) carry a comment that explicitly names only TDS discharge at Outfall 001.
